### Chadwick Installation Examples

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/chadwick_path

Examples demonstrating how to check for the Chadwick installation and conditionally run dependent functions.

```r
chadwick_path()
#> ! cwevent is not installed. Please see https://github.com/chadwickbureau/chadwick/releases for installation instructions. 

chadwick_is_installed()
#> ! cwevent is not installed. Please see https://github.com/chadwickbureau/chadwick/releases for installation instructions. 
#> [1] FALSE

chadwick_find_lib()
#> ! cwevent is not installed. Please see https://github.com/chadwickbureau/chadwick/releases for installation instructions. 
if (FALSE) { # \dontrun{
if (chadwick_ld_library_path()) {
  retrosheet_data(tempdir())
}
} # }
```

--------------------------------

### Live usage example for ncaa_pbp

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa_pbp

Example of calling ncaa_pbp with a game info URL from stats.ncaa.org. This is shown as a non-runnable example because live usage is behind Akamai bot protection and requires the optional chromote package and Google Chrome browser.

```r
ncaa_pbp(game_info_url = "https://stats.ncaa.org/contests/2167178/box_score")
```

--------------------------------

### Install baseballr with pak

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/articles/baseballr

Installs the baseballr package from GitHub using the pak package. First ensures pak is installed, then installs baseballr from the billpetti/baseballr repository.

```r
# You can install using the pak package using the following code:
if (!requireNamespace('pak', quietly = TRUE)){
  install.packages('pak')
}
pak::pak("billpetti/baseballr")
```

--------------------------------

### R: mlb_pbp_diff example with game_pk and timecodes

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_pbp_diff

Retrieves play-by-play differences for game 632970 between the given start and end timecodes. The output is a tibble with 50 rows and 141 columns, including pitch details and game metadata. The example is wrapped in try() and \donttest{} to avoid errors during testing.

```r
# \donttest{
  try(mlb_pbp_diff(game_pk = 632970, 
                   start_timecode = "20210808_231704", 
                   end_timecode = "20210808_233711"))
#> ── MLB Play-by-Play diff data from MLB.com ────────── baseballr 2.0.0 ──
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:30:46 UTC
#> # A tibble: 50 × 141
#>    game_pk game_date  index startTime       endTime isPitch type  playId
#>      <dbl> <chr>      <int> <chr>           <chr>   <lgl>   <chr> <chr> 
#>  1  632970 2021-08-08     2 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 0c67e…
#>  2  632970 2021-08-08     1 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 51631…
#>  3  632970 2021-08-08     0 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch e3edf…
#>  4  632970 2021-08-08     2 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 65870…
#>  5  632970 2021-08-08     1 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch d5fc8…
#>  6  632970 2021-08-08     0 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 0a6c9…
#>  7  632970 2021-08-08     4 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 506d0…
#>  8  632970 2021-08-08     3 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 24f33…
#>  9  632970 2021-08-08     2 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch aae6b…
#> 10  632970 2021-08-08     1 2021-08-08T23:… 2021-0… TRUE    pitch 63658…
#> # ℹ 40 more rows
#> # ℹ 133 more variables: pitchNumber <int>, details.description <chr>,
#> #   details.event <chr>, details.awayScore <int>,

### Example: mlb_seasons_all with sport_id = 1

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_seasons_all

Example call wrapped in try() to handle errors. The output shows a tibble with 152 rows and 21 columns, including season dates and wildcard status. The example is wrapped in \donttest{} to avoid running during package checks.

### Example: Retrieve starting batting order for game 566001

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_batting_orders

Calls mlb_batting_orders with game_pk=566001. The output is a tibble with 18 rows (9 per team) and columns including id, fullName, abbreviation, batting_order, batting_position_num, team, teamName, and teamID. The example is wrapped in \donttest{} to avoid CRAN test issues.

# \donttest{
  try(mlb_batting_orders(game_pk=566001))

### Get Play-By-Play Data for NCAA Baseball Games - ncaa_pbp()

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa

Fetches play-by-play data for a specific NCAA game. The example first retrieves a game info URL from the schedule, then passes it to ncaa_pbp().

```r
x <- ncaa_schedule_info(736, 2021)$game_info_url[2]
ncaa_pbp(game_info_url = x)
```

### ncaa_game_logs live usage examples

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa_game_logs

Four example calls demonstrating different player IDs, years, types, and spans. These are not runnable due to bot protection; they require chromote and Google Chrome fallback.

```r
ncaa_game_logs(player_id = 2649785, year = 2023, type = "pitching", span = "game")
```

```r
ncaa_game_logs(player_id = 2477974, year = 2023, type = "pitching", span = "career")
```

```r
ncaa_game_logs(player_id = 2680961, year = 2023, type = "batting", span = "game")
```

```r
ncaa_game_logs(player_id = 2486588, year = 2023, type = "batting", span = "career")
```

### Get game info with get_game_info_mlb

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/news/index.html

Retrieves additional game information such as weather and attendance for a set of game_pk IDs. The example maps over the first 10 game_pks from the previous example and selects game_date, venue_name, temperature, other_weather, and wind.

```R
map_df(.x = games$game_pk[1:10],
       ~get_game_info_mlb(.x)) %>%
  select(game_date, venue_name, temperature, other_weather, wind)

# A tibble: 10 x 5
   game_date  venue_name                 temperature other_weather wind
   <chr>      <chr>                      <chr>       <chr>         <chr>
 1 2019-05-01 Chickasaw Bricktown Ballp… 63          Cloudy        4 mph, R To L
 2 2019-05-01 Chickasaw Bricktown Ballp… 72          Cloudy        13 mph, R To L
 3 2019-05-01 ONEOK Field                77          Overcast      14 mph, In Fro…
 4 2019-05-01 ONEOK Field                74          clear         14 mph, In Fro…
 5 2019-05-01 Principal Park             54          Overcast      3 mph, In From…
 6 2019-05-01 Principal Park             53          Overcast      1 mph, Calm
 7 2019-05-01 Peoples Natural Gas Field  58          Overcast      7 mph, In From…
 8 2019-05-01 ARM & HAMMER Park          55          Overcast      5 mph, L To R
 9 2019-05-01 Durham Bulls Athletic Park 70          Partly Cloudy 7 mph, In From…
10 2019-05-01 Fifth Third Field          59          Cloudy        7 mph, R To L
```

### Example: statcast_search, run_expectancy_code, and linear_weights_savant

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/linear_weights_savant

Example workflow: search Statcast data for a batter, compute run expectancy, then generate linear weights. The output tibble shows linear weights above average and above outs for events.

```r
# \donttest{
 try({
   df <- statcast_search(start_date = "2016-04-06", end_date = "2016-04-15", 
                         playerid = 621043, player_type = 'batter') 
   df <- run_expectancy_code(df, level = "plate appearances")
   linear_weights_savant(df, level = "plate appearance")
 })
#> # A tibble: 5 × 3
#>   events   linear_weights_above_average linear_weights_above_outs
#>   <chr>                           <dbl>                     <dbl>
#> 1 home_run                         1                         1   
#> 2 double                           0.5                       0.5 
#> 3 single                           0.17                      0.17
#> 4 walk                             0                         0   
#> 5 outs                             0                         0   
# }
```

### Retrieve FanGraphs park factors with fg_park and fg_park_hand

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/fg_park

Examples from the fg_park documentation page showing how to retrieve park factors data from FanGraphs.com. The first example calls fg_park(2013) to get overall park factors, and the second calls fg_park_hand(2013) to get park factors broken down by handedness. Both are wrapped in try() and \donttest{} to handle potential errors and avoid CRAN test failures.

```r
# \donttest{
  try(fg_park(2013))
#> ── Park Factors data from FanGraphs.com ───────────── baseballr 2.0.0 ──
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:27:48 UTC
#> # A tibble: 30 × 16
#>    season home_team basic_5yr `3yr` `1yr` single double triple    hr
#>     <int> <chr>         <int> <int> <int>  <int>  <int>  <int> <int>
#>  1   2013 Angels           95    95    98    100     96     91    93
#>  2   2013 Orioles         103   102   103    101     99     86   108
#>  3   2013 Red Sox         105   104    98    103    114    105    96
#>  4   2013 White Sox       102   105   100     99     97     91   108
#>  5   2013 Indians         100    97    97    100    103     81   101
#>  6   2013 Tigers          101   103   106    101     98    126    98
#>  7   2013 Royals          101   102   104    101    103    114    93
#>  8   2013 Twins           101   103   101    102    101    106    98
#>  9   2013 Yankees         101   100   104    100     97     84   110
#> 10   2013 Athletics        97    97    95    99    100    105    93
#> # ℹ 20 more rows
#> # ℹ 7 more variables: so <int>, UIBB <int>, GB <int>, FB <int>,
#> #   LD <int>, IFFB <int>, FIP <int>
# }
```

```r
# \donttest{
  try(fg_park_hand(2013))

### Example: load_mlb_group_aliases with output

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/load_mlb_group_aliases

Example call wrapped in try() and donttest. The output shows a tibble with 96 rows and 8 columns, including league, group_id, source, source_id, name_kind, value, valid_from, and valid_to.

```r
# \donttest{
  try(load_mlb_group_aliases())
#> ── MLB group aliases from the SportsDataverse data repo ────────────────
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:28:13 UTC
#> # A tibble: 96 × 8
#>    league group_id  source source_id name_kind value valid_from valid_to

#>  3 mlb    mlb:al    espn   7         short_na… AL          1901       NA
#>  4 mlb    mlb:al    mlb    103       abbrevia… AL          1901       NA
#>  5 mlb    mlb:al    mlb    103       name      Amer…       1901       NA
#>  6 mlb    mlb:al    mlb    103       short_na… Amer…       1901       NA
#>  7 mlb    mlb:al-c… espn   2         abbrevia… ALC         1994       NA

### ncaa_lineups usage examples

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa_lineups

Example calls to ncaa_lineups with different game_info_url formats. Live usage reads stats.ncaa.org, which is behind Akamai bot protection and needs the optional chromote + Google Chrome browser fallback, so it is shown here rather than as a runnable example.

```r
ncaa_lineups(game_info_url = "https://stats.ncaa.org/contests/2167178/box_score")
ncaa_lineups(game_info_url = "https://stats.ncaa.org/game/index/4587474?org_id=528")
```

### Install baseballr from GitHub with pak

```r
# You can install using the pak package using the following code:
if (!requireNamespace('pak', quietly = TRUE)){
  install.packages('pak')
}
pak::pak("BillPetti/baseballr")
```

### statcast_search() examples for batter, pitcher, and daily searches

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/statcast_search

Three examples of statcast_search() calls: one for a batter (playerid 547180), one for a pitcher (playerid 664285), and one for all players over a date range. Each returns a tibble with 119 columns; the batter example returns 82 rows, the pitcher example 92 rows, and the daily example 250 rows. The calls are wrapped in try() and enclosed in \donttest{}.

```r
try(statcast_search(start_date = "2022-10-06", 
                      end_date = "2022-10-16", 
                      playerid = 547180, 
                      player_type = 'batter'))
```

```r
try(statcast_search(start_date = "2022-10-06", 
                      end_date = "2022-10-16", 
                      playerid = 664285, 
                      player_type = 'pitcher'))
```

```r
try(statcast_search(start_date = "2022-11-04", 
                      end_date = "2022-11-06"))
```

### Chadwick Installation Utility Functions

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/chadwick_path

Utility functions to help ensure that Chadwick is set up correctly. These functions check for Chadwick installation, find the shared library, and set the LD_LIBRARY_PATH environment variable.

```APIDOC
## chadwick_path()

### Description
Checks if Chadwick is installed and returns the path to the `cwevent` binary. If Chadwick is not installed, it prints a message and returns `NULL`.

### Usage
```r
chadwick_path()
```

### Value
If Chadwick is not installed, returns `NULL`. If installed, returns the path to the `cwevent` binary.

### Example
```r
chadwick_path()
#> ! cwevent is not installed. Please see https://github.com/chadwickbureau/chadwick/releases for installation instructions.
```
```

```APIDOC
## chadwick_is_installed()

### Description
Checks whether Chadwick is installed and returns a logical value indicating the result.

### Usage
```r
chadwick_is_installed()
```

### Value
Returns `TRUE` or `FALSE` indicating whether Chadwick is installed.

### Example
```r
chadwick_is_installed()
#> ! cwevent is not installed. Please see https://github.com/chadwickbureau/chadwick/releases for installation instructions.
#> [1] FALSE
```
```

```APIDOC
## chadwick_find_lib()

### Description
Finds the path to the Chadwick shared library. This function is mostly for internal use.

### Usage
```r
chadwick_find_lib()
```

### Value
Returns the path to the Chadwick shared library.

### Example
```r
chadwick_find_lib()
#> ! cwevent is not installed. Please see https://github.com/chadwickbureau/chadwick/releases for installation instructions.
```
```

```APIDOC
## chadwick_set_ld_library_path()

### Description
Sets the `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` environment variable to the Chadwick shared library path. This function is mostly for internal use.

### Usage
```r
chadwick_set_ld_library_path()
```

### Value
No documented return value.

### Example
No example provided in the source.
```

```APIDOC
## chadwick_ld_library_path()

### Description
Checks to find the Chadwick shared libraries and sets the `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` environment variable. If it returns `TRUE`, the `cwevent` command line program should work.

### Usage
```r
chadwick_ld_library_path()
```

### Value
Returns `TRUE` or `FALSE` indicating whether the `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` was set successfully.

### Example
```r
if (FALSE) { # \dontrun{
if (chadwick_ld_library_path()) {
  retrosheet_data(tempdir())
}
} # }
```
```

### Get minor league game_pks with get_game_pks_mlb

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/news/index.html

Queries MLB stats API for game_pk IDs for specified minor league levels on a given date. The example uses level_ids 11 (Triple-A) and 12 (Double-A) for 2019-05-01, then selects and displays the first 10 rows.

```R
games <- get_game_pks_mlb(date = '2019-05-01',
                          level_ids = c(11, 12))

games %>%
  select(game_pk, gameDate, teams.away.team.name, teams.home.team.name) %>%
  slice(1:10)

   game_pk             gameDate      teams.away.team.name  teams.home.team.name
1   579921 2019-05-01T16:05:00Z        Round Rock Express Oklahoma City Dodgers
2   579919 2019-05-01T03:33:00Z        Round Rock Express Oklahoma City Dodgers

4   584269 2019-05-01T03:33:00Z        Midland RockHounds        Tulsa Drillers
5   579571 2019-05-01T21:38:00Z      San Antonio Missions             Iowa Cubs
6   579570 2019-05-01T03:33:00Z      San Antonio Missions             Iowa Cubs

### Example: run_expectancy_code with statcast_search

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/run_expectancy_code

Example usage of run_expectancy_code with a statcast_search call for a specific batter. The example is wrapped in try() and \donttest{} to avoid CRAN test failures. The output shows a tibble with 159 rows and 134 columns, including pitch-level Statcast data and the appended run-expectancy columns.

```r
# \donttest{
 try({
   df <- statcast_search(start_date = "2016-04-06", end_date = "2016-04-15", 
                         playerid = 621043, player_type = 'batter') 
   run_expectancy_code(df, level = "plate appearances")
 })
#> # A tibble: 159 × 134

### Get play-by-play data with get_pbp_mlb

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/news/index.html

Fetches play-by-play data for a specific game using its game_pk. The example retrieves data for game 575589 (Gwinnett Stripers vs Charlotte Knights). The function returns a data frame with 131 columns; data availability varies by park and league level, and column names are mostly left as-is with possible duplicates.

```R
payload <- get_pbp_mlb(575589)
```

### Get Park Effects for NCAA Baseball Teams - ncaa_park_factor()

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa

Calculates park factors for an NCAA team over a range of years. The example uses team 736 for 2017-2019 with conference type.

```r
ncaa_park_factor(team_id = 736, years = c(2017:2019), type = "conference")
```

--------------------------------

### get_batting_orders function usage

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/get_batting_orders

Shows the function signature for get_batting_orders. The game_pk argument is required, and type defaults to 'starting' to return only the starting lineup; use 'all' to include all batters who appeared.

```r
get_batting_orders(game_pk, type = "starting")
```

### Get Fox Sports MLB odds with fox_mlb_odds

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/fox_mlb_odds

The function signature shows the required argument game_id, a Fox Bifrost event id. The example calls fox_mlb_odds with game_id "95687" and displays the resulting tibble with one row per team, including run line, to-win, and total odds. The output is empty when no market exists.

fox_mlb_odds(game_id)
```

 try(fox_mlb_odds("95687"))

### Basic ggspraychart usage

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ggspraychart

Minimal example call to generate a spray chart. It uses the default x and y coordinates and fills points by the 'events' variable.

```r
ggspraychart(df, x_value = "hc_x", y_value = "-hc_y", fill_value = "events")
```

### Example: label Statcast data and compute mean imputed

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/label_statcast_imputed_data

Example usage: searches Statcast data for May 1-2, 2017, labels it as imputed, and computes the mean of the `imputed` column. The output is `[1] 0.003550596`. Wrapped in `try()` and `\donttest{}` to avoid long-running tests.

```r
# \donttest{
  try({
    statcast_df <- statcast_search("2017-05-01", "2017-05-02")
    sc_df <- label_statcast_imputed_data(statcast_df)
    mean(sc_df$imputed)
  })
#> [1] 0.003550596
# }
```

### run_expectancy_code

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/run_expectancy_code

Documentation for the run_expectancy_code function, including its usage, arguments, return value, and an example.

```APIDOC
## run_expectancy_code(df, level = "plate appearance")

### Description
Generates run expectancy and related measures from Baseball Savant data, appending the computed columns to the input data frame.

### Function Signature
run_expectancy_code(df, level = "plate appearance")

### Parameters
- **df** (data.frame) - Required - A data frame generated from Baseball Savant.
- **level** (character) - Optional - Whether run expectancy is calculated at the plate appearance or pitch level. Defaults to "plate appearance".

### Return Value
Returns the input Baseball Savant data frame with run-expectancy columns appended. The appended columns are:

| col_name | types | description |
|----|----|----|
| final_pitch_game | numeric | 1 if the row is the last pitch of the game, 0 otherwise. |
| final_pitch_at_bat | numeric | 1 if the row is the last pitch of the plate appearance, 0 otherwise. |
| runs_scored_on_pitch | numeric | Runs that scored on the pitch (parsed from the play description, plus the batter on a home run). |
| bat_score_after | numeric | Batting team score after the pitch. |
| final_pitch_inning | numeric | 1 if the row is the last pitch of the half-inning, 0 otherwise. |
| bat_score_start_inning | numeric | Batting team score at the start of the half-inning. |
| bat_score_end_inning | numeric | Batting team score at the end of the half-inning. |
| cum_runs_in_inning | numeric | Cumulative runs scored in the half-inning up to the pitch. |
| runs_to_end_inning | numeric | Runs scored from the current state to the end of the half-inning. |
| count_base_out_state | character | Count, outs, and base-occupancy state string (pitch-level run expectancy). |
| avg_re | numeric | Average run expectancy for the current base-out (or count-base-out) state. |
| next_count_base_out_state | character | Base-out (or count-base-out) state of the following event. |
| next_avg_re | numeric | Average run expectancy for the next state (0 at the end of an inning). |
| change_re | numeric | Change in run expectancy between the current and next state. |
| re24 | numeric | RE24: change in run expectancy plus runs scored on the pitch. |

At the plate-appearance level the analogous state columns are named `base_out_state` and `next_base_out_state`.

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
try({
  df <- statcast_search(start_date = "2016-04-06", end_date = "2016-04-15", 
                        playerid = 621043, player_type = 'batter') 
  run_expectancy_code(df, level = "plate appearances")
})
#}
```
```

# \donttest{

### mlb_seasons_all

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_seasons_all

Documentation for the mlb_seasons_all function, including its parameters, return value, and an example call.

```APIDOC
## mlb_seasons_all

### Description
Retrieves season information for Major League Baseball (MLB) seasons, including dates for pre-season, regular season, post-season, and All-Star events, as well as qualification thresholds.

### Function Signature
```r
mlb_seasons_all(
  sport_id = 1,
  division_id = NULL,
  league_id = NULL,
  with_game_type_dates = TRUE
)
```

### Parameters
- **sport_id** (integer) - Optional - The sport ID to return season information for. Default is 1.
- **division_id** (integer) - Optional - The division ID to return season information for.
- **league_id** (integer) - Optional - The league ID to return season information for.
- **with_game_type_dates** (logical) - Optional - Whether to include game type dates in the returned data. Default is TRUE.

### Return Value
Returns a tibble with the following columns:
- **season_id** (character) - Season year identifier.
- **has_wildcard** (logical) - Whether the season has a wild card round.
- **pre_season_start_date** (character) - Pre-season start date.
- **season_start_date** (character) - Season start date.
- **regular_season_start_date** (character) - Regular season start date.
- **regular_season_end_date** (character) - Regular season end date.
- **season_end_date** (character) - Season end date.
- **offseason_start_date** (character) - Off-season start date.
- **off_season_end_date** (character) - Off-season end date.
- **season_level_gameday_type** (character) - Season-level Gameday data feed type.
- **game_level_gameday_type** (character) - Game-level Gameday data feed type.
- **qualifier_plate_appearances** (numeric) - Plate appearances per team game to qualify.
- **qualifier_outs_pitched** (integer) - Outs pitched per team game to qualify.
- **post_season_start_date** (character) - Post-season start date.
- **post_season_end_date** (character) - Post-season end date.
- **last_date1st_half** (character) - Last date of the first half.
- **all_star_date** (character) - All-Star Game date.
- **first_date2nd_half** (character) - First date of the second half.
- **pre_season_end_date** (character) - Pre-season end date.
- **spring_start_date** (character) - Spring training start date.
- **spring_end_date** (character) - Spring training end date.

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
try(mlb_seasons_all(sport_id = 1))
#}
```
```

--------------------------------

### mlb_pbp(game_pk, add_base_state = FALSE)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_pbp

Documentation for the mlb_pbp function, including usage, arguments, return value, and output columns.

```APIDOC
## mlb_pbp(game_pk, add_base_state = FALSE)

### Description
Acquires pitch-by-pitch data for Major and Minor League games from the MLB Stats API.

### Usage
```r
mlb_pbp(game_pk, add_base_state = FALSE)
```

### Arguments
- **game_pk** (numeric) - Required - The game primary key for which to acquire pitch-by-pitch data.
- **add_base_state** (logical) - Optional - If `TRUE`, appends per-event pre-pitch base-occupancy columns (`pre_on_1b`, `pre_on_2b`, `pre_on_3b`) reconstructed from the feed's runner-movement records. Defaults to `FALSE`.

### Value
Returns a tibble with over 100 columns of pitch-level data from the MLB Stats API. Column names are mostly left as-is and may contain duplicates. Data availability varies by park and league level; sensor data is often unavailable for minor league parks.

### Details
Both major and minor league pitch-by-pitch data can be pulled with this function.

### Output Columns
| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| game_pk | numeric | MLB game primary key. |
| game_date | character | Game date (YYYY-MM-DD). |
| index | integer | Index of the play event within the at-bat. |
| startTime | character | Event start timestamp (ISO 8601). |
| endTime | character | Event end timestamp (ISO 8601). |
| isPitch | logical | Whether the event is a pitch. |
| type | character | Play event type (e.g. 'pitch', 'action'). |
| playId | character | Unique play event identifier (UUID). |
| pitchNumber | integer | Pitch number within the at-bat. |
| details.description | character | Pitch/event description (e.g. 'Swinging Strike'). |
| details.event | character | Event name for non-pitch actions. |
| details.awayScore | integer | Away score recorded at the event. |
| details.homeScore | integer | Home score recorded at the event. |
| details.isScoringPlay | logical | Whether the event is a scoring play. |
| details.hasReview | logical | Whether the event was reviewed. |
| details.code | character | Pitch/result code (e.g. 'S', 'C', 'B'). |
| details.ballColor | character | Gameday ball color rgba string. |
| details.isInPlay | logical | Whether the ball was put in play. |
| details.isStrike | logical | Whether the pitch was a strike. |
| details.isBall | logical | Whether the pitch was a ball. |
| details.call.code | character | Umpire call code. |
| details.call.description | character | Umpire call description. |
| count.balls.start | integer | Ball count before the pitch. |
| count.strikes.start | integer | Strike count before the pitch. |
| count.outs.start | integer | Out count before the pitch. |
| player.id | integer | Player id for action-event participant. |
| player.link | character | API relative link to the action player. |
| pitchData.strikeZoneTop | numeric | Top of the batter strike zone (feet). |
| pitchData.strikeZoneBottom | numeric | Bottom of the batter strike zone (feet). |
| details.fromCatcher | logical | Whether the event originated from the catcher. |
| pitchData.coordinates.x | numeric | Pitch x pixel coordinate (Gameday). |
| pitchData.coordinates.y | numeric | Pitch y pixel coordinate (Gameday). |
| hitData.trajectory | character | Batted ball trajectory. |
| hitData.hardness | character | Batted ball hardness. |
| hitData.location | character | Fielding position where the ball was hit. |
| hitData.coordinates.coordX | numeric | Batted ball landing x coordinate. |
| hitData.coordinates.coordY | numeric | Batted ball landing y coordinate. |
| actionPlayId | character | Identifier of an associated action play. |
| details.eventType | character | Event type slug for non-pitch actions. |
| details.runnerGoing | logical | Whether a runner was going on the pitch. |
| position.code | character | Fielding position code for the player. |
| position.name | character | Fielding position name. |
| position.type | character | Fielding position type. |
| position.abbreviation | character | Fielding position abbreviation. |
| battingOrder | character | Batting order slot. |
| atBatIndex | character | At-bat index within the game (factor). |
| result.type | character | Result type (e.g. 'atBat'). |
| result.event | character | At-bat result event (e.g. 'Strikeout'). |
| result.eventType | character | At-bat result event slug. |
| result.description | character | Narrative description of the at-bat result. |
| result.rbi | integer | Runs batted in on the at-bat. |
| result.awayScore | integer | Away score after the at-bat. |
| result.homeScore | integer | Home score after the at-bat. |
| about.atBatIndex | integer | At-bat index (numeric). |
| about.halfInning | character | Half inning ('top' or 'bottom'). |
```

--------------------------------

### fg_team_batter function usage example

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/fg_team_batter

Shows the full function call with all default arguments. Use this as a template to customize the scraping parameters.

```r
fg_team_batter(
  age = "",
  pos = "all",
  stats = "bat",
  lg = "all",
  qual = "y",
  startseason = "2023",
  endseason = "2023",
  startdate = "",
  enddate = "",
  month = "0",
  hand = "",
  team = "0,ts",
  pageitems = "1000",
  pagenum = "1",
  ind = "0",
  rost = "0",
  players = "",
  type = "8",
  postseason = "",
  sortdir = "default",
  sortstat = "WAR"
)
```

### Example: Scrape batter data from 2015-05-10 to 2015-06-20

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/bref_daily_batter

Calls bref_daily_batter with a date range and wraps it in try() to handle potential errors. The output shows a tibble with 658 rows and 30 columns of batting statistics.

```r
# \donttest{
  try(bref_daily_batter(t1="2015-05-10", t2="2015-06-20"))
#> ── MLB Daily Batter data from baseball-reference.com ───────────────────

### ncaa_lineups

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa_lineups

Documentation for the ncaa_lineups function, including usage, arguments, return value, and examples.

```APIDOC
## ncaa_lineups(game_info_url = NULL, ...)

### Description
Retrieves the batting lineups for a given NCAA game using its `game_info_url`. The function parses the game's `individual_stats` box scores to return each team's batting order.

### Usage
```r
ncaa_lineups(game_info_url = NULL, ...)
```

### Arguments
- **game_info_url** (character) - Required - The unique game info URL for the NCAA game.
- **...** - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function like `httr`.

### Value
Returns a tibble with one row per batter in batting order, containing the following columns:
- **player_name** (character) - Player name.
- **position** (character) - Fielding position (the box score "P" column).
- **batting_order** (integer) - Spot in the batting order (box-score row order).
- **team_name** (character) - Team name (from the box-score totals row).
- **player_id** (integer) - stats.ncaa.org player identifier.
- **player_url** (character) - Full stats.ncaa.org URL for the player page.
- **slug** (character) - Relative stats.ncaa.org URL for the player page.

### Details
Live usage reads `stats.ncaa.org`, which is behind Akamai bot protection and may require the optional `chromote` + Google Chrome browser fallback.

### Examples
```r
ncaa_lineups(game_info_url = "https://stats.ncaa.org/contests/2167178/box_score")
ncaa_lineups(game_info_url = "https://stats.ncaa.org/game/index/4587474?org_id=528")
```
```

--------------------------------

### Calculate pitcher FIP and wOBA with fip_plus()

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr

Generate wOBA-based stats and FIP for pitchers using fip_plus(). The example pulls daily pitcher data for April 5-30, 2015, computes FIP and wOBA against, selects relevant columns, sorts by innings pitched descending, and shows the top 10.

```r
bref_daily_pitcher("2015-04-05", "2015-04-30") %>% 
  fip_plus() %>% 
  dplyr::select(season, Name, IP, ERA, SO, uBB, HBP, HR, FIP, wOBA_against, wOBA_CON_against) %>%
  dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(IP)) %>% 
  head(10)
```

### load_mlb_team_group_seasons

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/load_mlb_team_group_seasons

Documentation for the load_mlb_team_group_seasons function, including usage, arguments, return value, and an example.

```APIDOC
## load_mlb_team_group_seasons

### Description
Loads MLB team league and division memberships by season from the SportsDataverse data repo. Returns one row per MLB team per season, including league and division information.

### Usage
```r
load_mlb_team_group_seasons(
  seasons = most_recent_mlb_season(),
  ...,
  dbConnection = NULL,
  tablename = NULL
)
```

### Arguments
- **seasons** (vector of integers) - Optional - A vector of 4-digit seasons, or `TRUE` for every published season. (Min: 1901)
- **...** (additional arguments) - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function that writes the data into a database.
- **dbConnection** (DBIConnection) - Optional - A `DBIConnection` object, as returned by `DBI::dbConnect()`.
- **tablename** (character) - Optional - The name of the data table within the database.

### Value
A `baseballr_data` tibble with columns:
- **league** (character) - League key.
- **season** (integer) - Season.
- **team_id** (character) - ESPN team id where ESPN covers the team, otherwise the league's own id.
- **team_id_source** (character) - Id system of `team_id` (e.g. `espn`, `mlb`, `ncaa_org`).
- **team_name** (character) - Team name as of that season.
- **subdivision_id** (character) - SportsDataverse subdivision group id; `NA` where the level does not apply.
- **conference_id** (character) - SportsDataverse conference group id (for MLB, the American or National League).
- **division_id** (character) - SportsDataverse division group id; `NA` where the level does not apply.
- **source** (character) - Source the membership came from.
- **sources_agree** (logical) - Whether a second source agrees; `NA` when only one source covers the season.
- **notes** (character) - Notes, e.g. the league's own team id.

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
  try(load_mlb_team_group_seasons(seasons = 2013))
#> ── MLB team group seasons from the SportsDataverse data repo ───────────
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:28:16 UTC
#> # A tibble: 30 × 11
#>    league season team_id team_id_source team_name         subdivision_id
#>    <chr>   <int> <chr>   <chr>          <chr>             <chr>         
#>  1 mlb      2013 1       espn           Baltimore Orioles NA            
#>  2 mlb      2013 10      espn           New York Yankees  NA            
#>  3 mlb      2013 11      espn           Oakland Athletics NA            
#>  4 mlb      2013 12      espn           Seattle Mariners  NA            
#>  5 mlb      2013 13      espn           Texas Rangers     NA            
#>  6 mlb      2013 14      espn           Toronto Blue Jays NA            
#>  7 mlb      2013 15      espn           Atlanta Braves    NA            
#>  8 mlb      2013 16      espn           Chicago Cubs      NA            
#>  9 mlb      2013 17      espn           Cincinnati Reds   NA            
#> 10 mlb      2013 18      espn           Houston Astros    NA            
#> # ℹ 20 more rows
#> # ℹ 5 more variables: conference_id <chr>, division_id <chr>,
#> #   source <chr>, sources_agree <lgl>, notes <chr>
# }
```
```

### mlb_game_info(game_pk)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_game_info

Documentation for the mlb_game_info function, including usage, arguments, return value, and an example.

```APIDOC
## mlb_game_info(game_pk)

### Description
Retrieves additional game information for major and minor league games, including weather, official scorer, attendance, and other supplemental details for a given game.

### Method
R function call

### Endpoint
`mlb_game_info(game_pk)`

### Parameters
#### Arguments
- **game_pk** (numeric) - Required - The unique game_pk identifier for the game.

### Return Value
Returns a tibble with the following columns:

| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| game_date | character | Game date (YYYY-MM-DD). |
| game_pk | numeric | Unique game identifier. |
| venue_name | character | Stadium name. |
| venue_id | integer | Venue ID. |
| temperature | character | Game-time temperature (degrees F). |
| other_weather | character | Weather condition description. |
| wind | character | Wind speed and direction. |
| attendance | character | Reported game attendance. |
| start_time | character | First-pitch local start time. |
| elapsed_time | character | Total elapsed game time (H:MM). |
| game_id | character | Human-readable game ID slug. |
| game_type | character | Game type code (R, P, etc.). |
| home_sport_code | character | Home sport code (always 'mlb'). |
| official_scorer | character | Official scorer name. |
| date | character | Long-form game date label. |
| status_ind | character | Game status code. |
| home_league_id | integer | Home team league ID. |
| gameday_sw | character | Gameday data type code. |

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
  try(mlb_game_info(game_pk = 566001))
#> ── MLB Game Info data from MLB.com ────────────────── baseballr 2.0.0 ──
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:30:31 UTC
#> # A tibble: 1 × 18
#>   game_date  game_pk venue_name venue_id temperature other_weather wind 
#>   <chr>        <dbl> <chr>         <int> <chr>       <chr>         <chr>
#> 1 2019-04-29  566001 Citi Field     3289 51          Cloudy        10 m…
#> # ℹ 11 more variables: attendance <chr>, start_time <chr>,
#> #   elapsed_time <chr>, game_id <chr>, game_type <chr>,
#> #   home_sport_code <chr>, official_scorer <chr>, date <chr>,
#> #   status_ind <chr>, home_league_id <int>, gameday_sw <chr>
# }
```

### ncaa_schedule_info

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa_schedule_info

Documentation for the ncaa_schedule_info function, including usage, arguments, return value, and example.

```APIDOC
## ncaa_schedule_info

### Description
Retrieves the schedule and results for a specified NCAA baseball team for a given season. Returns a data frame with game details including dates, opponents, results, scores, and game URLs.

### Usage
```r
ncaa_schedule_info(team_id = NULL, year = NULL, pbp_links = FALSE, ...)
```

### Arguments
- **team_id** (integer) - Required - The team's unique NCAA id.
- **year** (integer) - Required - The season (e.g., use 2016 for the 2015-2016 season).
- **pbp_links** (logical) - Optional - Logical parameter to run process for scraping play-by-play URLs for each game.
- **...** - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function like httr.

### Value
A data frame with the following fields: date, opponent, result, score, innings (if more than regulation), and the URL for the game itself.

### Details
```r
try(ncaa_schedule_info(team_id = 736, year = 2019))
```

### Returned Data Frame Columns
| col_name | types | description |
|----------|-------|-------------|
| year | integer | Season (4-digit year). |
| season_id | integer | stats.ncaa.org season identifier. |
| date | character | Game date. |
| home_team | character | Home team name. |
| home_team_id | integer | Home team NCAA id. |
| home_team_conference | character | Home team conference name. |
| home_team_conference_id | integer | Home team conference id. |
| home_team_slug | character | Relative stats.ncaa.org url for the home team. |
| home_team_division | integer | Home team NCAA division (1, 2, 3). |
| away_team | character | Away team name. |
| away_team_id | integer | Away team NCAA id. |
| away_team_conference | character | Away team conference name. |
| away_team_conference_id | integer | Away team conference id. |
| away_team_slug | character | Relative stats.ncaa.org url for the away team. |
| away_team_division | integer | Away team NCAA division (1, 2, 3). |
| neutral_site | character | Neutral-site venue (when not hosted by either team). |
| result | character | Win/loss/tie result for `team_id`. |
| score | character | Final score (e.g. "7-3"). |
| innings | character | Innings played when other than regulation (extras). |
| slug | character | Relative stats.ncaa.org url for the game. |
| game_info_url | character | Full stats.ncaa.org box-score url for the game. |
| contest_id | integer | stats.ncaa.org contest (game) identifier. |
```

### espn_mlb_game_probables(game_id)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/espn_mlb_game_probables

Documentation for the espn_mlb_game_probables function, which retrieves probable starting pitchers for an ESPN MLB game.

```APIDOC
## espn_mlb_game_probables(game_id)

### Description
Returns the probable / announced starting pitchers for a game, one row per team, parsed from the ESPN game-summary `header.competitions.competitors` `probables` block. Available for upcoming and completed games.

### Usage
```r
espn_mlb_game_probables(game_id)
```

### Arguments
- **game_id** (character or numeric) - Required - ESPN event identifier.

### Value
A `baseballr_data` tibble with one row per team's probable starter. Columns:
- **game_id** (integer) - Unique ESPN game/event identifier.
- **season** (integer) - Season (4-digit year).
- **season_type** (integer) - ESPN season type (1=pre, 2=regular, 3=post).
- **team_id** (integer) - Unique ESPN team identifier.
- **team_abbreviation** (character) - Short team abbreviation (e.g. 'NYY').
- **team_display_name** (character) - Full team display name.
- **home_away** (character) - Venue label for the team ('home' or 'away').
- **athlete_id** (integer) - Unique ESPN athlete identifier (pitcher).
- **athlete_full_name** (character) - Pitcher full name.
- **athlete_display_name** (character) - Pitcher display name.
- **athlete_short_name** (character) - Pitcher short display name.
- **jersey** (character) - Jersey number.
- **position** (character) - Position abbreviation (SP / RP / P).
- **throws** (character) - Throwing handedness (L / R).
- **headshot_href** (character) - Headshot image URL.
```

### load_ncaa_baseball_team_group_seasons

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/load_ncaa_baseball_team_group_seasons

Documentation for the load_ncaa_baseball_team_group_seasons function, including its signature, parameters, return value, and an example.

```APIDOC
## load_ncaa_baseball_team_group_seasons

### Description
Loads NCAA baseball team division and conference memberships by season from the SportsDataverse data repo. Returns one row per NCAA baseball team per season, with the division and conference it played in that season.

### Function Signature
```r
load_ncaa_baseball_team_group_seasons(
  seasons = most_recent_ncaa_baseball_season(),
  ...,
  dbConnection = NULL,
  tablename = NULL
)
```

### Parameters
- **seasons** (vector of integers) - Optional - A vector of 4-digit seasons (the spring year), or `TRUE` for every published season. Minimum: 2010. Default: most recent NCAA baseball season.
- **...** (additional arguments) - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function that writes the data into a database.
- **dbConnection** (DBIConnection) - Optional - A `DBIConnection` object, as returned by `DBI::dbConnect()`.
- **tablename** (character) - Optional - The name of the data table within the database.

### Return Value
A `baseballr_data` tibble with the following columns:
- **league** (character) - League key.
- **season** (integer) - Season.
- **team_id** (character) - ESPN team id where ESPN covers the team, otherwise the league's own id.
- **team_id_source** (character) - Id system of `team_id` (e.g. `espn`, `mlb`, `ncaa_org`).
- **team_name** (character) - Team name as of that season.
- **subdivision_id** (character) - SportsDataverse subdivision group id; `NA` where the level does not apply.
- **conference_id** (character) - SportsDataverse conference group id (for MLB, the American or National League).
- **division_id** (character) - SportsDataverse division group id; `NA` where the level does not apply.
- **source** (character) - Source the membership came from.
- **sources_agree** (logical) - Whether a second source agrees; `NA` when only one source covers the season.
- **notes** (character) - Notes, e.g. the league's own team id.

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
  try(load_ncaa_baseball_team_group_seasons(seasons = 2025))
#> ── NCAA baseball team group seasons from the SportsDataverse data repo ─
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:30:13 UTC
#> # A tibble: 943 × 11
#>    league        season team_id team_id_source team_name  subdivision_id
#>    <chr>          <int> <chr>   <chr>          <chr>      <chr>         
#>  1 ncaa_baseball   2025 10      ncaa_org       UAH        ncaa_baseball…
#>  2 ncaa_baseball   2025 100     ncaa_org       Cal State… ncaa_baseball…
#>  3 ncaa_baseball   2025 1000    ncaa_org       Carson-Ne… ncaa_baseball…
#>  4 ncaa_baseball   2025 1001    ncaa_org       Catawba    ncaa_baseball…
#>  5 ncaa_baseball   2025 1004    ncaa_org       Central A… ncaa_baseball…
#>  6 ncaa_baseball   2025 1009    ncaa_org       Central O… ncaa_baseball…
#>  7 ncaa_baseball   2025 101     ncaa_org       CSUN       ncaa_baseball…
#>  8 ncaa_baseball   2025 1010    ncaa_org       Central W… ncaa_baseball…
#>  9 ncaa_baseball   2025 1013    ncaa_org       Charlesto… ncaa_baseball…
#> 10 ncaa_baseball   2025 1014    ncaa_org       Col. of C… ncaa_baseball…
#> # ℹ 933 more rows
#> # ℹ 5 more variables: conference_id <chr>, division_id <chr>,
#> #   source <chr>, sources_agree <lgl>, notes <chr>
# }
```
```

### mlb_team_info

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_team_info

Documentation for the mlb_team_info function, including usage, arguments, return value, and an example.

```APIDOC
## mlb_team_info(team_id = NULL, season = NULL, sport_id = NULL)

### Description
Returns team information for a given team, season, or sport. The function queries the MLB Stats API and returns a tibble with detailed team data.

### Usage
```r
mlb_team_info(team_id = NULL, season = NULL, sport_id = NULL)
```

### Arguments
- **team_id** (integer) - Optional - The team_id to return team data for.
- **season** (integer) - Optional - The season to return team data for the given year.
- **sport_id** (integer) - Optional - The sport_id to return a directory of team data for a particular club in a sport.

### Value
Returns a tibble with the following columns:

| col_name | types | description |
|----|----|----|
| all_star_status | character | All-star status flag. |
| team_id | integer | Team MLBAM ID. |
| team_full_name | character | Full team name. |
| link | character | API link to the team. |
| season | integer | Season year. |
| team_code | character | Internal team code. |
| file_code | character | File code abbreviation. |
| team_abbreviation | character | Team abbreviation. |
| team_name | character | Short team name. |
| location_name | character | Team location (city). |
| first_year_of_play | character | First year the franchise played. |
| short_name | character | Short display name. |
| franchise_name | character | Franchise name. |
| club_name | character | Club name. |
| active | logical | Whether the team is active. |
| spring_league_id | integer | Spring league MLBAM ID. |
| spring_league_name | character | Spring league name. |
| spring_league_link | character | API link to the spring league. |
| spring_league_abbreviation | character | Spring league abbreviation. |
| venue_id | integer | Home venue MLBAM ID. |
| venue_name | character | Home venue name. |
| venue_link | character | API link to the venue. |
| spring_venue_id | integer | Spring training venue MLBAM ID. |
| spring_venue_link | character | API link to the spring venue. |
| league_id | integer | League MLBAM ID. |
| league_name | character | League name. |
| league_link | character | API link to the league. |
| division_id | integer | Division MLBAM ID. |
| division_name | character | Division name. |
| division_link | character | API link to the division. |
| sport_id | integer | Sport MLBAM ID. |
| sport_link | character | API link to the sport. |
| sport_name | character | Sport name (e.g., Major League Baseball). |

### Examples
```r
# \donttest{
  try(mlb_team_info(team_id = 147))
#> ── MLB Team Info data from MLB.com ────────────────── baseballr 2.0.0 ──
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:31:02 UTC
#> # A tibble: 1 × 33
#>   all_star_status team_id team_full_name   link         season team_code
#>   <chr>             <int> <chr>            <chr>         <int> <chr>    
#> 1 N                   147 New York Yankees /api/v1/tea…   2026 nya      
#> # ℹ 27 more variables: file_code <chr>, team_abbreviation <chr>,
#> #   team_name <chr>, location_name <chr>, first_year_of_play <chr>,
#> #   short_name <chr>, franchise_name <chr>, club_name <chr>,
#> #   active <lgl>, spring_league_id <int>, spring_league_name <chr>,
#> #   spring_league_link <chr>, spring_league_abbreviation <chr>,
#> #   venue_id <int>, venue_name <chr>, venue_link <chr>,
#> #   spring_venue_id <int>, spring_venue_link <chr>, league_id <int>, …
# }
```

### get_probables_mlb(game_pk)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/get_probables_mlb

This function retrieves probable starting pitchers and the home plate umpire for a given MLB game using its unique game_pk identifier.

```APIDOC
## get_probables_mlb(game_pk)

### Description
Retrieves the probable starting pitchers and home plate umpire for a given MLB game identified by its unique game_pk identifier.

### Usage
```r
get_probables_mlb(game_pk)
```

### Arguments
- **game_pk** (integer) - Required - The unique game_pk identifier for the game.

### Value
Returns a tibble that includes probable starting pitchers and the home plate umpire for the requested game_pk.
```

--------------------------------

### mlb_seasons(sport_id = 1, with_game_type_dates = TRUE)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_seasons

Documentation for the mlb_seasons function, including usage, arguments, return value, and example.

```APIDOC
## mlb_seasons(sport_id = 1, with_game_type_dates = TRUE)

### Description
Returns MLB season information for a given sport ID, including key dates for pre-season, regular season, post-season, and off-season, as well as qualification thresholds.

### Usage
```r
mlb_seasons(sport_id = 1, with_game_type_dates = TRUE)
```

### Arguments
- **sport_id** (integer) - The sport_id to return season information for.
- **with_game_type_dates** (logical) - Whether to return season information with game type dates.

### Value
Returns a tibble with the following columns:

| Column | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| season_id | character | Season year identifier. |
| has_wildcard | logical | Whether the season has a wild card round. |
| pre_season_start_date | character | Pre-season start date. |
| pre_season_end_date | character | Pre-season end date. |
| season_start_date | character | Season start date. |
| spring_start_date | character | Spring training start date. |
| spring_end_date | character | Spring training end date. |
| regular_season_start_date | character | Regular season start date. |
| last_date1st_half | character | Last date of the first half. |
| all_star_date | character | All-Star Game date. |
| first_date2nd_half | character | First date of the second half. |
| regular_season_end_date | character | Regular season end date. |
| post_season_start_date | character | Post-season start date. |
| post_season_end_date | character | Post-season end date. |
| season_end_date | character | Season end date. |
| offseason_start_date | character | Off-season start date. |
| off_season_end_date | character | Off-season end date. |
| season_level_gameday_type | character | Season-level Gameday data feed type. |
| game_level_gameday_type | character | Game-level Gameday data feed type. |
| qualifier_plate_appearances | numeric | Plate appearances per team game to qualify. |
| qualifier_outs_pitched | integer | Outs pitched per team game to qualify. |

### Examples
```r
# \donttest{
try(mlb_seasons(sport_id = 1))
#> ── MLB Seasons data from MLB.com ──────────────────── baseballr 2.0.0 ──
#> ℹ Data updated: 2026-09-30 14:30:56 UTC
#> # A tibble: 1 × 21
#>   season_id has_wildcard pre_season_start_date pre_season_end_date
#>   <chr>     <lgl>        <chr>                 <chr>              
#> 1 2026      TRUE         2026-01-01            2026-02-19         
#> # ℹ 17 more variables: season_start_date <chr>,
#> #   spring_start_date <chr>, spring_end_date <chr>,
#> #   regular_season_start_date <chr>, last_date1st_half <chr>,
#> #   all_star_date <chr>, first_date2nd_half <chr>,
#> #   regular_season_end_date <chr>, post_season_start_date <chr>,
#> #   post_season_end_date <chr>, season_end_date <chr>,
#> #   offseason_start_date <chr>, off_season_end_date <chr>, …
# }
```

### playername_lookup(id)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/chadwick_player_name_lu

Documentation for the playername_lookup function, including usage, arguments, return value, and examples.

```APIDOC
## Function: playername_lookup(id)

### Description
Queries the Chadwick Bureau's public register of baseball players to look up a player by ID and return associated IDs across different systems of record.

### Usage
```r
playername_lookup(id)
```

### Arguments
- **id** (integer or character) - Required - A player ID in a baseball database, cross-referenced through the Chadwick Bureau's public register.

### Value
A data frame of baseball players and the various IDs associated with them in different systems of record.

### Return Fields
- **name_first** (character) - Player first name.
- **name_last** (character) - Player last name.
- **name_given** (character) - Player full given (legal) name.
- **name_suffix** (character) - Name suffix (e.g. Jr., Sr., III).
- **name_nick** (character) - Player nickname.
- **birth_year** (integer) - Year of birth.
- **mlb_played_first** (integer) - First MLB season as a player.
- **key_mlbam** (integer) - MLB Advanced Media (MLBAM) player ID.
- **key_retro** (character) - Retrosheet player ID.
- **key_bbref** (character) - Baseball-Reference player ID.
- **key_fangraphs** (integer) - FanGraphs player ID.

### Examples
```r
# \donttest{
  try(playername_lookup(4885))
  try(playername_lookup("kaaihki01"))
# }
```
```

### Coloring by pitch type

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/articles/plotting_statcast

Uses statcast_pitch_colors() to get Baseball Savant's pitch-type palette and plots pitch locations colored by pitch type.

```r
pal <- statcast_pitch_colors()
pitch_pal <- setNames(pal$color, pal$pitch_type)

betts_16 |>
  dplyr::filter(!is.na(pitch_type)) |>
  ggplot(aes(x = plate_x, y = plate_z, color = pitch_type)) +
  geom_point(alpha = 0.5) +
  scale_color_manual(values = pitch_pal) +
  coord_fixed() +
  theme_bw()
```

### get_ncaa_baseball_pbp(game_info_url, game_pbp_url, raw_html_to_disk, raw_html_path, read_from_file, file, ...)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/get_ncaa_baseball_pbp

Retrieves play-by-play data for an individual NCAA baseball game. Accepts game info and play-by-play URLs, with options to write raw HTML to disk or read from a file. Returns a data frame with play-by-play details.

```APIDOC
## [FUNCTION] get_ncaa_baseball_pbp

### Description
Retrieves play-by-play data for an individual NCAA baseball game using the legacy baseballr function. The function accepts game info and play-by-play URLs, with options to write raw HTML to disk or read from a file.

### Method
FUNCTION

### Endpoint
get_ncaa_baseball_pbp(game_info_url, game_pbp_url, raw_html_to_disk, raw_html_path, read_from_file, file, ...)

### Parameters
#### Arguments
- **game_info_url** (character) - Required - The url for the game's boxscore data. This can be found using the ncaa_schedule_info function.
- **game_pbp_url** (character) - Required - The url for the game's play-by-play data. This can be found using the ncaa_schedule_info function.
- **raw_html_to_disk** (logical) - Optional - Write raw html to disk (saves as `{game_pbp_id}`.html in `raw_html_path` directory).
- **raw_html_path** (character) - Optional - Directory path to write raw html.
- **read_from_file** (logical) - Optional - Read from raw html on disk.
- **file** (character) - Optional - File with full path to read raw html.
- **...** (any) - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function like httr.

### Request Example
```r
get_ncaa_baseball_pbp(
  game_info_url = "https://example.com/boxscore",
  game_pbp_url = "https://example.com/pbp"
)
```

### Response
#### Success Response (data frame)
- **game_date** (character) - Game date (NA on the redesigned page; use ncaa_schedule_info()).
- **location** (character) - Venue / conditions line when present.
- **attendance** (logical) - Reported attendance (NA on the redesigned page).
- **inning** (character) - Inning number.
- **inning_top_bot** (character) - Half-inning ("top" or "bot").
- **score** (character) - Running score (away-home) after the play.
- **batting** (character) - Batting team name.
- **fielding** (character) - Fielding team name.
- **description** (character) - Play description text.
- **game_pbp_url** (character) - stats.ncaa.org play-by-play url for the game.
- **game_pbp_id** (integer) - stats.ncaa.org play-by-play (contest) identifier.

#### Response Example
```r
# A data frame with columns as described above
```
```

### ncaa_team_player_stats(team_id, year, type, ...)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/ncaa_team_player_stats

Documentation for the ncaa_team_player_stats function, including usage, arguments, return value, and an example call.

```APIDOC
## ncaa_team_player_stats(team_id, year, type, ...)

### Description
Scrapes batting, pitching, or fielding statistics for a given NCAA baseball team (Division I, II, or III) from stats.ncaa.org and returns a tibble.

### Usage
```r
ncaa_team_player_stats(
  team_id,
  year = most_recent_ncaa_baseball_season(),
  type = "batting",
  ...
)
```

### Arguments
- **team_id** (numeric) - Required - The numerical ID that the NCAA website uses to identify a team.
- **year** (character) - Optional - The season for which data should be returned, in the form of "YYYY". Years currently available: 2013-2017.
- **type** (character) - Optional - A string indicating whether to return "batting", "pitching", or "fielding" statistics.
- **...** - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function like httr.

### Value
A data frame (tibble) with the following variables. The exact stat columns vary by `type` (batting / pitching / fielding); the table below shows the batting (`type = "batting"`) columns.

| col_name      | types     | description                                   |
|---------------|-----------|-----------------------------------------------|
| year          | integer   | Season (4-digit year).                        |
| team_name     | character | Team name.                                    |
| team_id       | numeric   | Team NCAA id.                                 |
| conference_id | integer   | Conference identifier.                        |
| conference    | character | Conference name.                              |
| division      | numeric   | NCAA division (1, 2, 3).                      |
| player_id     | integer   | stats.ncaa.org player identifier.             |
| player_url    | character | Full stats.ncaa.org url for the player page.  |
| player_name   | character | Player name.                                  |
| Yr            | character | Academic class/year (Fr, So, Jr, Sr).         |
| Pos           | character | Primary fielding position.                    |
| Jersey        | character | Jersey number (the site's "#" column).        |
| GP            | numeric   | Games played.                                 |
| GS            | numeric   | Games started.                                |
| BA            | numeric   | Batting average.                              |
| OBPct         | numeric   | On-base percentage.                           |
| SlgPct        | numeric   | Slugging percentage.                          |
| R             | numeric   | Runs scored.                                  |
| AB            | numeric   | At-bats.                                      |
| H             | numeric   | Hits.                                         |
| 2B            | numeric   | Doubles.                                      |
| 3B            | numeric   | Triples.                                      |
| TB            | numeric   | Total bases.                                  |
| HR            | numeric   | Home runs.                                    |
| RBI           | numeric   | Runs batted in.                               |
| BB            | numeric   | Walks (bases on balls).                       |
| HBP           | numeric   | Hit by pitch.                                 |
| SF            | numeric   | Sacrifice flies.                              |
| SH            | numeric   | Sacrifice hits (bunts).                       |
| K             | numeric   | Strikeouts.                                   |
| DP            | numeric   | Grounded into double plays (site's "OPP DP"). |
| CS            | numeric   | Caught stealing.                              |
| Picked        | numeric   | Times picked off.                             |
| SB            | numeric   | Stolen bases.                                 |
| RBI2out       | numeric   | Runs batted in with two outs.                 |

### Details
Live usage (reads `stats.ncaa.org`, which is behind Akamai bot protection and needs the optional `chromote` + Google Chrome browser fallback, so it is shown here rather than as a runnable example):

```r
ncaa_team_player_stats(team_id = 234, year = 2023, type = "batting")
```
```

### fg_team_pitcher function usage example

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/fg_team_pitcher

Shows the default call to fg_team_pitcher with all arguments set to their defaults, scraping team pitcher leaderboard data for the 2023 season. The function returns a data frame of team pitching statistics.

```r
fg_team_pitcher(
  age = "",
  pos = "all",
  stats = "pit",
  lg = "all",
  qual = "y",
  startseason = "2023",
  endseason = "2023",
  startdate = "",
  enddate = "",
  month = "0",
  hand = "",
  team = "0,ts",
  pageitems = "1000",
  pagenum = "1",
  ind = "0",
  rost = "0",
  players = "",
  type = "8",
  postseason = "",
  sortdir = "default",
  sortstat = "WAR"
)
```

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/retrosheet_data

Shows the function signature with default arguments. Requires the Chadwick CLI's cwevent application to be installed and available from the command line. On Unix platforms it uses the system() interface; on Windows it uses the shell() interface.

```r
retrosheet_data(
  path_to_directory = NULL,
  years_to_acquire = most_recent_mlb_season() - 1,
  sequence_years = FALSE
)

### fg_park and fg_park_hand usage examples

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/fg_park

Two R function calls showing how to scrape park factors for a given season. fg_park returns overall park factors, while fg_park_hand returns park factors broken down by handedness. Both take a single argument, yr, representing the season.

```R
fg_park(yr)
```

```R
fg_park_hand(yr)
```

### espn_college_baseball_week_ranking

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/espn_college_baseball_week_ranking

Documentation for the espn_college_baseball_week_ranking function, including usage, arguments, and an example.

```APIDOC
## espn_college_baseball_week_ranking

### Description
Retrieves ESPN college baseball week ranking information for a given ranking ID, week, and season.

### Usage
```r
espn_college_baseball_week_ranking(ranking_id, week, season)
```

### Arguments
- **ranking_id** (integer) - Required - The ranking ID to retrieve.
- **week** (integer) - Required - The week number of the ranking.
- **season** (integer) - Required - The season year.

### Value
A tibble containing the week ranking data. If the request fails, an error is thrown with details about the HTTP response code.

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
  try(espn_college_baseball_week_ranking(ranking_id = 1, week = 5, season = 2025))
# }
```
```

### most_recent_college_baseball_season()

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/most_recent_college_baseball_season

Function to get the year of the most recent NCAA college baseball season.

```APIDOC
## most_recent_college_baseball_season()

### Description
Returns the year of the most recent NCAA college baseball season. The function accounts for the season's timeline (February through June) and returns the current year from February onward, and the prior year in January.

### Usage
```r
most_recent_college_baseball_season()
```

### Arguments
None.

### Value
An integer indicating the year of the most recent NCAA college baseball season.

### Examples
```r
most_recent_college_baseball_season()
#> [1] 2026
```
```

--------------------------------

### ncaa_baseball_pbp(game_info_url, game_pbp_url, raw_html_to_disk, raw_html_path, read_from_file, file, ...)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/get_ncaa_baseball_pbp

Alias for get_ncaa_baseball_pbp() with identical behavior. Retrieves play-by-play data for an individual NCAA baseball game.

```APIDOC
## [FUNCTION] ncaa_baseball_pbp

### Description
Alias for get_ncaa_baseball_pbp() with identical behavior. Retrieves play-by-play data for an individual NCAA baseball game.

### Method
FUNCTION

### Endpoint
ncaa_baseball_pbp(game_info_url, game_pbp_url, raw_html_to_disk, raw_html_path, read_from_file, file, ...)

### Parameters
#### Arguments
- **game_info_url** (character) - Required - The url for the game's boxscore data. This can be found using the ncaa_schedule_info function.
- **game_pbp_url** (character) - Required - The url for the game's play-by-play data. This can be found using the ncaa_schedule_info function.
- **raw_html_to_disk** (logical) - Optional - Write raw html to disk (saves as `{game_pbp_id}`.html in `raw_html_path` directory).
- **raw_html_path** (character) - Optional - Directory path to write raw html.
- **read_from_file** (logical) - Optional - Read from raw html on disk.
- **file** (character) - Optional - File with full path to read raw html.
- **...** (any) - Optional - Additional arguments passed to an underlying function like httr.

### Request Example
```r
ncaa_baseball_pbp(
  game_info_url = "https://example.com/boxscore",
  game_pbp_url = "https://example.com/pbp"
)
```

### Response
#### Success Response (data frame)
- **game_date** (character) - Game date (NA on the redesigned page; use ncaa_schedule_info()).
- **location** (character) - Venue / conditions line when present.
- **attendance** (logical) - Reported attendance (NA on the redesigned page).
- **inning** (character) - Inning number.
- **inning_top_bot** (character) - Half-inning ("top" or "bot").
- **score** (character) - Running score (away-home) after the play.
- **batting** (character) - Batting team name.
- **fielding** (character) - Fielding team name.
- **description** (character) - Play description text.
- **game_pbp_url** (character) - stats.ncaa.org play-by-play url for the game.
- **game_pbp_id** (integer) - stats.ncaa.org play-by-play (contest) identifier.

#### Response Example
```r
# A data frame with columns as described above
```
```

### Fetch MLB batter leaders with fg_batter_leaders

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/fg_batter_leaders

This example calls fg_batter_leaders for the 2023 season. The output is a tibble with 1,457 rows and 447 columns, including player identifiers, team, and many batting statistics. The function is wrapped in try() and \donttest{} to avoid long-running tests.

```r
# \donttest{
  try(fg_batter_leaders(startseason = 2023, endseason = 2023))
#> ── MLB Player Batting Leaders data from FanGraphs.com ──────────────────

### espn_college_baseball_player_overview

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/espn_college_baseball_player_overview

Documentation for the espn_college_baseball_player_overview function, including its signature, parameters, and return value structure.

```APIDOC
## espn_college_baseball_player_overview

### Description
Retrieves an overview of a college baseball athlete's profile from ESPN, including statistics, upcoming games, recent game results, headlines, and fantasy outlook.

### Function Signature
```r
espn_college_baseball_player_overview(
  athlete_id,
  season = most_recent_college_baseball_season(),
  ...
)
```

### Parameters
- **athlete_id** (character or numeric) - Required - ESPN athlete identifier.
- **season** (numeric) - Optional - Season year. Defaults to the most recent college baseball season.
- **...** - Optional - Additional arguments; currently unused.

### Return Value
A named list of data frames: `Statistics`, `NextGame`, `Last5Games`, `Headlines`, `FantasyOutlook`.

#### Statistics
- **col_name** (character) - Column name (varies).

#### NextGame
- **id** (character) - Id.
- **date** (character) - Date in YYYY-MM-DD format.
- **name** (character) - Display name.
- **short_name** (character) - Short display name.

#### Last5Games
- **col_name** (character) - Column name (varies).

#### Headlines
- **headline** (character) - News headline.
- **description** (character) - Long-form description text.
- **published** (character) - Publication timestamp (ISO 8601).

#### FantasyOutlook
- **col_name** (character) - Column name (varies).
```

### load_ncaa_baseball_pbp()

Loads cleaned NCAA baseball play-by-play data from the baseballr data repo.

```APIDOC
## load_ncaa_baseball_pbp()

### Description
Loads cleaned NCAA baseball play-by-play data from the baseballr data repo.

### Function Signature
`load_ncaa_baseball_pbp(...)`

### Usage
`load_ncaa_baseball_pbp(...)`

### Parameters
No parameters are explicitly documented in the source.

### Return Value
A data frame containing cleaned NCAA baseball play-by-play data.

### Notes
- This function is part of the baseballr data repository loader functions.
- It loads cleaned play-by-play data for NCAA baseball from the baseballr data repo.

### Example
```r
# Load cleaned NCAA baseball play-by-play data
load_ncaa_baseball_pbp()
```
```

### mlb_pbp(game_pk)

Source: https://billpetti.github.io/baseballr/reference/mlb_pbp

Documentation for the mlb_pbp function, which retrieves play-by-play data for a given MLB game.

```APIDOC
## Function: mlb_pbp(game_pk)

### Description
Retrieves play-by-play data for a given MLB game from MLB.com.

### Usage
```r
mlb_pbp(game_pk)
```

### Parameters
- **game_pk** (numeric) - Required - The game primary key (game ID) for the MLB game.

### Return Value
A tibble (data frame) containing play-by-play data with columns such as `game_pk`, `game_date`, `index`, `startTime`, `endTime`, `isPitch`, `type`, `playId`, and many more (151 columns total).

### Example
```r
# \donttest{
  try(mlb_pbp(game_pk = 632970))
# }
```

### Notes
- The function is part of the `baseballr` package (version 2.0.0).
- The output includes detailed pitch and play information, including pitch number, event details, scores, and flags for scoring plays, reviews, and pitch outcomes.
```
